The bounded S4 discovery reused [D13](../drafts/Yin_D_G0013.md#automatic-multiple-and-frobenius-supply) and its actual coefficient and trace-order interfaces, without finding an additional supply mechanism.

The latter excludes the specified short binary auxiliaries, but the [actual trace-order test](../drafts/Yin_D_G0013.md#trace-order-supply-test) supplies no implication from that radial concentration to a short codeword or logarithmic residue-image rank.

## Positive entropy dispersed through genuine quotient flows

2026-10-15T17:51.
Root retains Yin's structural question: can finite total algebraic entropy remain dispersed through infinitely many indispensable positive-entropy quotient layers?
Operation: clarify S1's existing finite-capture question and its connections to the [C155 next-round plan](#c155-next-round-strategy-2026-10-16); disposition paused -> paused.
Yin authorized documentation and handoff only.
This is a strategy interpretation of already retained mechanisms plus concrete future questions, not a new theorem, proof review, counterexample or execution assignment.
The existing planned S3/S4 roles and general S8 coverage remain unchanged.

### Existing mechanisms and exact meanings

The responsible inputs are [the Task's algebraic-entropy contract](../tasks/Yin_T_G0001_v1.md#algebraic-entropy-contract), [L1's finite-capture assessment](../ledger/Yin_L_G0001_v1.md#lehmer-route-assessment), the [Addition Theorem interface](../audits/literature/DGB16/Yin_U002.md#dgb16-u002), and the [attainment/stabilization interface](../audits/literature/DGB16/Yin_U004.md#dgb16-u004).
Their existing source corrections and inspection limits remain unchanged.
The following are existing mechanisms, not newly obtained progress:

- For actual algebraic flows with positive entropies e_j and finite sum, their direct sum has entropy sum e_j.
  Every finite subset has finite summand support and cannot attain that full sum.
  Such a family already has e_j tending to zero and therefore refutes a uniform positive gap.
  Conversely zero gap permits choosing0<e_j<2^(-j), giving exactly this nonattaining direct sum.
  This conditional equivalence, already in L1, needs no exact-spectrum theorem.
  Finite total entropy alone does not supply the needed positive summands.
- For an increasing sequence of invariant subgroups V_n whose restriction entropies approach a finite total h, addition identifies each increment with the entropy of the ACTUAL quotient flow on V_n/V_(n-1).
  Splitting is unnecessary.
  A uniform positive gap eta would allow at most floor(h/eta) positive increments, forcing entropy stabilization; zero increments may still occur indefinitely.
  Infinite strict growth with finite total would itself produce positive quotient entropies tending to zero.
  Invoking eta here is the existing conditional mechanism, not a proof of eta.
- Finite-set attainment H(phi,F)=h(phi), capture by a finitely orbit-generated subgroup V(phi,F), and generation of the entire group V(phi,F)=G are different assertions.
  A realizing finite set gives capture; the accepted finite-entropy realization interface supplies a suitable finite realizing set inside a capturing finitely generated flow, not necessarily the originally chosen generating set.
  Capture permits an arbitrarily large zero-entropy quotient.
  It asserts neither finite rank of the whole group nor a finite measurable generating partition.

For h(phi)<infinity, a convenient restatement of the capture obligation is to find a finite set F whose actual invariant orbit subgroup satisfies

    V(phi,F)=<phi^n(F): n>=0>,
    h_alg(phi induced on G/V(phi,F))=0.

This is the existing finite-capture criterion through addition, not a weaker solved substitute.
Constructing a flow of finite positive entropy for which every such quotient has positive entropy would refute the gap; merely postulating a summable positive-entropy family does not construct one.

### Missing arithmetic input and controls

The question is which divisions are available in the original arithmetic category.
For an irreducible rational polynomial P, the rational flow Q[t]/(P) is a simple Q[t]-module.
Its complex eigenvalue decomposition does not supply separate rational invariant flows for each conjugate.
The separate summands log^+ abs(alpha_j) in the monic Mahler formula therefore cannot be assigned independently as small entropy layers.
Genuine invariant subgroups and induced quotient maps must be exhibited before the Addition Theorem is used.
Finite-time trajectory directions or arbitrary spectral pieces are not automatically such quotients.

L1 already gives a split weighted-length countermodel: additivity, continuity under directed unions and a Noetherian coefficient ring do not force finite capture for an arbitrary real-valued length.
Actual algebraic entropy has additional arithmetic weights.
A proposed argument must use those weights or their relations, rather than silently assume a minimum positive increment.
A positive gap would be a lower bound on positive values, not an assertion that all entropy values are integer multiples of one unit.

Retain the existing calibration controls: an infinite zero-entropy tail may survive after full entropy is captured; a directed union of invariant zero-entropy flows cannot acquire positive entropy by continuity; and the cyclic time-dilation model with psi^k equal to k copies of phi has h(psi)=h(phi), not h(phi)/k.
Original field degrees, copies, primitive normalization and true quotient entropy remain part of every proposed construction.

### Concrete questions for possible later selection

| Future question | First concrete object or check | Useful outcome and stop boundary |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Can an apparent fine decomposition be realized by genuine arithmetic quotients? | Specify one invariant filtration, the induced quotient endomorphisms, their rational/integer realizations and the applicable entropy formula. Distinguish quotient entropy from finite-time growth or individual conjugate logarithms. | An explicit structural restriction or a correctly normalized realization with a new quantitative bound. Stop if the small increments are only assigned numerical weights or require an unproved near-zero entropy family. |
| Can a positive-entropy arithmetic block supply a controlled whole-field certificate? | Start with one actual irreducible companion/quotient flow, identify the integral relations coupling its conjugates, and state the certificate's full norm income and representation/transfer costs. | A new arithmetic inequality with all rank/degree dependence exposed, or a scoped obstruction. Neither abstract simplicity nor a restatement of the desired gap counts as that inequality. |
| Could a nonsplit extension or direct-limit construction give an unconditional nonattaining flow? | Specify the group and map independently of a preselected small-entropy sequence, then seek both a finite total-entropy bound and a proof of positive residual quotient entropy for EVERY finite orbit-generated subgroup. | A genuine construction satisfying both obligations would be decisive. Non-splitting itself supplies neither obligation; arbitrary weights, unchecked limits or hidden time/multiplicity normalization are stopping diagnostics. |

These are future selection questions, not newly registered conjectures or claims of feasible constructions.
The [fixed2d prescribed-value test](#c155-fixed-2d-energy-four-test-2026-10-16) is the preferred first diagnostic within the second question, without displacing the parallel direct-logarithm branch or authorizing execution.
A bounded attempt may produce a useful example or a partial obstruction, but its exact scope must be retained.
The unrestricted finite-capture question remains the final criterion; the next useful advance must identify actual arithmetic information that the generic length argument lacks.

### Connection to the two C155 routes

[C155](../claims/Yin_C_G0155_v1.md#original-polynomial-return) couples all original embeddings through an auxiliary order-integral value with full norm at least2^d on its ACTUAL totally-real trace domain.
Its normalized log2 is certificate income, NOT a proved minimum entropy increment of the original flow or of every genuine quotient.

  The old C151 covariance inequality still has its different left-hand quantity.
- **Value-preserving representatives:** a legal change B_tilde=B3+P R keeps the value at every original conjugate and hence its full norm.
  This preserves the coupled arithmetic information while changing circle representation costs; it does not split the flow or create new entropy.
  A cheap representative, paid transfer and automatic small-measure coverage remain open.
  Changing the auxiliary value instead requires a new income argument.

The structural viewpoint therefore helps choose what information a successful certificate must retain.
It supplements the short direct-logarithm diagnostic and parallel value-preserving representative attempt; it does not replace either by an already solved finite-generation principle.
General algebraic input coverage, including inputs outside the totally-real trace class, remains with the unchanged overall strategy.

## Small-boundary factors: checked source conclusions and paused arithmetic questions

This section owns the strategy consequences and future selection questions, not another proof or a new Claim assessment.

### Checked source input and its inspection boundary

The inspected artifacts are [Lin99](../literature/pdf/Lin99.pdf), the [Lin95 author version](../literature/pdf/Lin95--author-ps-converted.pdf), and [SW91](../literature/pdf/SW91.pdf).
Lin99 Theorem6.7 states that a compact metric homeomorphism with SBP admits, for every a!=b and every epsilon>0, a continuous onto factor pi with pi(a)!=pi(b) and h_top(pi(X))<epsilon.
Once SBP is assumed, this implication needs no extra minimality, finite-dimensionality, aperiodicity or finite-entropy hypothesis.
It does not assert that finite entropy alone implies SBP.
The selected pair's factor may have zero entropy; for a positive-entropy source, the checked countable-separation argument yields some arbitrarily small strictly positive-entropy factors.
The entropy-interpolation mechanism is retained at the exact [Lin95 source scope](../audits/literature/Lin95/readme.md#entropy-interpolation).

The [proof reconstruction](../audits/literature/Lin99/Yin_U001.md#proof-reconstruction) checks the periodic-point completion and uses the standard closed-relation quotient topology, correcting the fiber Hausdorff-metric sentence acknowledged by Lin99.
Its inspection is a single-agent source check with stated foundational inputs, not a second independent review, formal verification or a whole-paper audit of all three sources.
Lin95's author version has not been compared passage by passage with the journal edition.
The full mean-dimension/embedding and transversality/Anosov proofs remain outside that check.

The transferable mechanism is uniform orbit control and a summable budget for boundary refinements.
The inequality bounding the constructed factor entropy by the sum of coding entropies is an upper bound on construction cost, not a decomposition of the original flow into independent indispensable positive-entropy layers.
Pairwise low-cost separation also supplies no uniform number, regularity or arithmetic complexity of observations separating all points at once.

### Continuous quotients are not automatically genuine algebraic quotient layers

A continuous factor is a genuine quotient in the topological-dynamical category.
Its defining closed invariant equivalence relation need not respect addition, so it need not be a group quotient or the dual of a discrete abelian flow.
The [genuine-quotient question above](#real-quotient-entropy-dispersion-2026-10-16) instead requires actual invariant subgroups, induced quotient endomorphisms and the correct algebraic-entropy normalization.
Neither small continuous factors nor differences between their entropy values can be inserted into the algebraic Addition Theorem without that realization.

Retain the source check's binary full-shift calibration: it has arbitrarily small positive-entropy continuous factors, while its discrete dual shift has a finite set F={0,e_0} with |T_n(phi,F)|=2^n and H(phi,F)=h_alg(phi)=log2.
Thus small continuous factors coexist with finite-set attainment and do not by themselves refute the G0001 generation/attainment property.
This is an existing standard-example comparison, not a new counterexample or a new small-positive-Mahler family.

### Pontryagin duality: the residual quotient is on the kernel side

For an invariant subgroup V of a discrete abelian group G, the exact sequence is

\[
0\longrightarrow\widehat{G/V}=V^\perp
\longrightarrow\widehat G\longrightarrow\widehat V
\longrightarrow0.
\]

The restriction flow on V corresponds to the image factor on the compact side; the residual quotient flow on G/V corresponds to the invariant kernel subgroup V^perp.
Under the applicable Bridge Theorem, the finite-capture target h_alg(G/V(phi,F))=0 asks for zero entropy in that kernel after the finite-character-orbit factor retains the entropy.
Lin99 bounds an image factor's entropy instead, and even an algebraic small-image factor would not settle the residual-kernel target.

The domain limits in [Lin99-U001's duality comparison](../audits/literature/Lin99/Yin_U001.md#algebraic-category-boundary) are mandatory.
The Task permits arbitrary endomorphisms, whereas Lin99 assumes a homeomorphism.
For a merely forward-invariant V, an automorphism of G can induce a noninvertible quotient endomorphism, so the countable-automorphism form of the Bridge Theorem cannot be substituted without checking its hypotheses.
The general endomorphism bridge is reported in DGB16, but the existing DGB12 original-source access limitation remains unchanged; this handoff supplies no new original-proof inspection.
The kernel/image identification as an exact-sequence statement does not depend on auditing that entropy theorem.

### C155: an exceptional-set logarithmic target, not a consequence of SBP

The adopted [C155 income](../claims/Yin_C_G0155_v1.md#original-polynomial-return) remains Lambda_P=log Norm_K(h)/d>=log2 only on its ACTUAL monic irreducible reciprocal nontorsion integer-unit domain with totally real trace field.
It is auxiliary norm income, not a lower bound for each positive-entropy quotient layer.
The existing targets I_P<=log2-eta and Lambda_P-I_P<=E(m(P)), with E(m)->0 uniformly in degree, remain unresolved as stated in the [two-branch plan](#c155-next-round-strategy-2026-10-16).

The source reading suggests an OPTIONAL sufficient way to organize the upper-cost obligation.
For a future declared input P, write nu_P=(1/d)sum_i delta_(alpha_i/|alpha_i|), retaining every original conjugate and its multiplicity.
Choose an explicit circle logarithm q_P, initially log Q_P for the existing B3, with a specified regularization and its costs if necessary.
A proposed target is a set E_P on the circle for every P in an explicitly declared class with 0<m(P)<m_0, with m_0 and eta>0 uniform over that class and independent of d, such that

\[
q_P\le\log2-\eta\quad\hbox{on }E_P^c,
\qquad
\int_{E_P}(q_P-(\log2-\eta))_+\,d\nu_P\le\eta/2.
\]

The actual original-root comparison would still need a separately proved bound

\[
\Lambda_P\le\int q_P\,d\nu_P+R_P,
\qquad R_P\le R(m(P)),\qquad R(m)\longrightarrow0,
\]

with R independent of P except through m(P), and all radial, regularization, moving-test, frequency and normalization expenses included.
These displays are FUTURE SUFFICIENT TARGETS, not estimates proved by SBP, numerical observations or new C155 consequences.
No q_P regularization, E_P, positive-tail value or R_P was constructed or evaluated in this documentation turn.
The conjugate-angle measure has not been shown to be an invariant measure of an applicable SBP system.
Small Haar measure of E_P alone controls neither its nu_P mass nor the logarithm's amplitude there.
This possible decomposition supplements the existing Haar-margin/one-sided-discrepancy targets; it is not a newly imposed necessary condition on every successful logarithmic method.

| Existing future branch | What this reading suggests | Still-unproved obligations |

E_(2d)(h)<4 alone is still insufficient, and an energy or circle-contact obstruction to supremum<2 does not automatically rule out a paid logarithmic estimate.

### Future discrimination, continuation and stopping

| Does a proposed low-entropy construction preserve the required category? | An explicit invariant subgroup/induced quotient flow, a closed invariant group congruence, or a quantified arithmetic return from the nonlinear observation. | Only an arbitrary continuous factor, assigned numerical entropy weights, or an assumed family of small positive algebraic entropies. |

## Fixed 2d window: the prescribed C155 value and the energy-four test

2026-10-15T18:07.
The specification below retains its original planning scope.
Its first four-input execution is now retained in [J97](../computations/programs/Yin_J_G0097/readme.md#finite-results), with the [bounded outcome and recommendations](#fixed-2d-first-round-outcome-2026-10-18) kept separately from the original unexecuted plan.
Root records Yin's instruction to retain the concrete question, first-round test design, continuation criteria and stop conditions without performing any calculation.
Operation: specify the first bounded diagnostic within the [genuine-quotient certificate question](#real-quotient-entropy-dispersion-2026-10-16) and the [value-preserving C155 branch](#c155-next-round-strategy-2026-10-16); disposition paused -> paused.
The parallel direct-logarithm diagnostic remains in the plan.
At the time of this entry it was a FUTURE test specification: no input matrices or energies had been computed, no mathematical program or J had been created, and no investigation or review was started or queued.

### Exact input, target and unresolved question

Use only the ACTUAL [C155 domain](../claims/Yin_C_G0155_v1.md#original-polynomial-return): monic irreducible reciprocal nontorsion integer-unit P of degree d=2r whose actual trace field is totally real.
Its original quotient R_P=Z[t,t^(-1)]/(P) carries multiplication by t.
Retain the SAME prescribed h=e^2+f^2+g^2 from the C153v2 trace quotients; C155 supplies Norm_K(h)>=2^d.
This is auxiliary norm income, not the entropy of a newly invented quotient layer.
It does not cover all reciprocal inputs.

The first question is whether the arithmetic structure of this specific h forces its minimum scalar coefficient energy in the ordinary window0,...,2d-1 to be at least4.
If it does not, identify which actual input structure permits crossing4 and whether the subsequent circle/transfer costs can retain a useful margin.
No answer to either question is claimed here.

Let r_h be the exact integer coordinate column of h in the original power basis of Z[alpha], and define the integer remainder matrix

    A_(2d)=[rem_P(1),rem_P(t),...,rem_P(t^(2d-1))].

The subscript2d counts columns; the allowed ordinary degree is at most2d-1 and the actual support width is at most2d-1.
Its first d columns form I_d.
A rational scalar representative f(t)=sum_(k=0)^(2d-1)x_k t^k is legal precisely when A_(2d)x=r_h.
This equality fixes the value at EVERY original conjugate, not just one numerical root.

The existing [C16 least-representative interface](../claims/Yin_C_G0016_v1.md#weighted-quotient-lattice-certificate), specialized to the Euclidean coefficient norm and the PRESCRIBED residue, supplies

    E_(2d)(h)=min_{x in Q^(2d): A_(2d)x=r_h} sum_k x_k^2
             =r_h^T(A_(2d)A_(2d)^T)^(-1)r_h,
    x_*=A_(2d)^T(A_(2d)A_(2d)^T)^(-1)r_h.

These are reused formulas, not new results of this plan.
The new unknown is the relation between the actual C155 vector r_h and this quadratic form.
Do NOT replace r_h by a shortest nonzero integer vector over the whole residue lattice, change h, or substitute a convenient abstract triple for the actual Frobenius data.
Any such replacement would require its own norm-income argument.

### Why the screening threshold is4

For a scalar representative f, normalized Haar Parseval gives integral_T abs(f)^2=sum x_k^2.
A circle bound ||f||_infinity<2 therefore requires coefficient energy<4.
Consequently an EXACT E_(2d)(h)>=4 excludes EVERY legal representative in this window from the strict-supremum-below2 certificate on that input.
Equality4 also fails the strict threshold.

Conversely E_(2d)(h)<4 only passes a necessary screen.
It proves neither ||f||_infinity<2 nor a cheap original-root logarithmic estimate.
The scalar SQUARED-energy threshold4 must not be confused with the original positive symbol Q_P's FIRST-moment threshold2.
Neither circle positivity nor lag cancellation is inherited by the least-energy representative.
The old C151 covariance estimate still cannot be transferred to the new scalar norm without a valid interface.

A useful stronger target would be an actual legal representative with ||f||_infinity<=2 exp(-eta), eta>0 independent of d, on a proved input class.
The already used maximum-principle return would then give

    log2<=log Norm_K(h)/d
         <=log ||f||_infinity+((2d-1)/d)m(P),

and hence m(P)>=eta/2 on that coverage class.
No such representative, uniform eta or automatic small-measure coverage has been obtained.
All rational normalization and original roots are retained; growing denominators are neither a free gain nor an additional unproved log-q penalty.

### First-round procedure, only after authorized resumption

1. Declare a bounded list of already verified actual inputs.
   Begin with one existing reciprocal control to check implementation, then a small stated set of eligible higher-degree inputs.
   Verify the full C155 hypotheses for each; fixed positive-measure controls are not evidence of arbitrarily small positive measure.
   The input list and any native computation must be retained under the applicable J rules when that work is actually authorized, not fabricated in this documentation turn.
2. Construct the actual trace defects, h, r_h and A_(2d) with exact integer/rational arithmetic.
   Check the original-order coordinate identity and A_(2d)x_*=r_h exactly.
   Approximate equality at a selected root is insufficient.
   Retain P, the target column, matrix, rational representative and actual denominators so the test can be reproduced.
3. Determine the sign of E_(2d)(h)-4 exactly.
   No floating-point near-threshold result is classified as a certificate.
   A genuine E<4 example refutes the universal barrier over the full tested input domain; a finite sample of E>=4 values proves no uniform barrier.
4. For inputs crossing4, check the actual circle costs before enlarging the sample.
   Examine correlations and a certified supremum bound, and test the immutable contact at original circle conjugates: if some original abs(alpha_i)=1 has abs(h_i)>=2, NO value-preserving representative can have circle supremum<2 on that input, regardless of window.
   Certify any asserted algebraic comparison.
   Failure of the PARTICULAR least-energy representative's supremum test alone does not exclude other representatives in its affine residue fiber.
5. Look for an explanation of a strict margin or obstruction in terms of the prescribed h and actual input arithmetic.
   Return that explanation with its exact range.
   Do not expand an unchanged scan or redefine the objective merely to obtain another small numerical value.

### Continue, redirect or stop: exact result scopes

| Possible outcome of later work | What it would establish | Next decision |
| --- | --- | --- |
| One verified actual input has E_(2d)(h)<4 | The all-input energy-four barrier is false; this target has some representation freedom. | Permit a small follow-up on its structural cause and circle costs. Do not infer near-one coverage or launch a large scan from this example alone. |
| A class has a structural E<=4-delta margin with controlled circle/transfer expenses | A concrete mechanism is available beyond the old minimum-norm formula. | Increase effort only while the margin, dependence on total measure, full costs and degree-uniformity are being resolved. Energy margin alone remains insufficient. |
| A proved class-wide E_(2d)(h)>=4 bound | No input in THAT class admits the strict-supremum-below2 certificate with the SAME h in THIS window. | Close this specified attempt on that class. Preserve direct logarithmic control and other legitimately priced representatives as separate questions. |
| One input has E>=4, or many samples do | An obstruction for each certified input only. | Do not promote it to a universal or near-one theorem; seek a structural reason or stop repetitive sampling. |
| An original circle conjugate has abs(h_i)>=2 | Strict supremum<2 is impossible for every representative of this same value on THAT input, even in longer windows. | Stop its supremum branch. This does not rule out direct logarithmic estimates or a different auxiliary value with a new income proof. |
| Only x_* has an excessive supremum, or only the reused least-norm formula is recovered | Neither a whole-fiber obstruction nor a new arithmetic result. | Keep the quantifier local; identify a different controlled representative/mechanism or stop this bounded attempt without claiming a theorem. |

No negative result for the fixed2d window automatically extends to all longer fixed-multiple windows, to logarithmic rather than supremum estimates, or to all auxiliary values.
Enlarging the window is a separate justified choice with its actual width charge; allowing its multiple of d to grow without paying that cost is not a successful threshold crossing.
A single example away from the small-measure regime does not refute a target asserted only for sufficiently small m.

The useful first-round deliverable is an exact prescribed-target threshold certificate plus a structural explanation or a carefully scoped obstruction.
The next stronger deliverable is a strict net margin uniform in degree and automatically available on its claimed small-measure input class.
The unrestricted Task and the general-input coverage outside C155's trace-field domain remain unresolved.

## Integer coefficients and coupling of methods

**Task-specific research emphasis.** Yin directs all G0001 main strategies and substrategies to emphasize integer coefficients and explore the coupling and mutual influence of analytic estimates, algebraic restrictions and structural constructions.
The common reference is the original primitive integer polynomial and its legitimate equivalent representations.
Use the established noncyclotomic monic-unit hard core where applicable, preserving all original conjugates and the total Mahler measure.
Degrees, coefficient heights and ranks remain unrestricted in the final Task.

Keep integer recurrences, annihilators, orders/lattices, primitive contents, norms/resultants, congruences and residue factors visible in an analytic model.
Rational bases, real weights, smoothing and complex coefficient relaxations remain useful tools; retain their denominators, realization requirements and full costs when returning to the original integer input.
A continuous relaxation is a comparison or a candidate until the needed integer interface is proved.

Investigate both directions of influence.
Integer relations can constrain spectra, Fourier amplitudes, collision laws and optimizer geometry; small analytic expense or hypothetical near-one behavior can force coefficient growth, congruence restrictions or residue-degree escape; an integral basis, completion or graph change can alter both the arithmetic data and the full analytic cost.
Prefer questions that connect these effects on the same actual input over separate improvements that have no justified combination.

| Coupled interface | Existing starting point and current question |
| --- | --- |
| Integer auxiliary and primitive norm versus coefficient energy and signed correlation | [L237](../claims/Yin_C_G0570_v1.md#uniform-auxiliary-margin-fixed-scope) and [L318](../claims/Yin_C_G0600_v1.md#statement): positive surplus exists; seek a relation controlling the actual aggregate correlation/whole-conjugate expense. |
| Integer orbit relations and ideal structure versus weighted returns and neutral geometry | [S2](Yin_S_G0002.md#route) and [S7](Yin_S_G0007.md#route): retain the original law, actual recurrence and jointly weighted cost. |
| Integral realization, graph/basis changes and complete factors versus determinant/entropy cost | [S3](Yin_S_G0003.md#route) and [S6](Yin_S_G0006.md#route): explain the effect of the structural move on every scalar factor and its arithmetic and analytic expenses. |
| Local residue/valuation mass versus height, exponent and norm upper bounds | [C504](../claims/Yin_C_G0504_v1.md#growing-residue-fixed-scope) and [S8](Yin_S_G0008.md#route): use both the near-one mass restriction and the unresolved same-exponent income requirement. |

For a consequential candidate, state the integer restriction being used, the analytic/structural observable it could control, the direction of the proposed implication, and the input/degree/height costs that remain.
A partial coupling estimate, a discriminating actual-input example or a precise failed implication is useful progress; a complete gap mechanism is not an admission requirement for exploration.
Continuous controls, including the [C211 nonintegral family](../claims/Yin_C_G0211_v1.md#fixed-surplus-vanishing-measure), can expose missing arithmetic information without becoming counterexamples in the integer Task.

This emphasis governs the retained parallel allocation.
It changes no fixed result, review scope, route hierarchy or Task contract.

<a id="parallel-exploration-policy"></a>
## Parallel exploration choice

**Yin's instruction, 2026-10-09.** Candidate directions should not be completely suspended merely because their possible breakthrough is invisible from the current position.
Keep room for parallel bold experiments and conjectural leaps, alongside justified incremental progress.
This Task-specific choice supersedes the earlier narrow allocation and route-level pauses below; it does not change the unrestricted Lehmer contract or the scope of any mathematical obstruction.

S_G0001 coordinates the active arithmetic routes S_G0004/S_G0008 together with active growth/orbit routes S_G0002/S_G0007, Fourier route S_G0005, completion/graph route S_G0006 and exploratory determinant route S_G0003.
Arithmetic keeps primary attention, while other directions receive genuine parallel or rotating opportunities as available execution resources permit.
A new construction, speculative analogy, conjectural relationship, toy model or exploratory computation can be a legitimate starting point before a complete scalar return, uniform estimate or demonstrable gain is known.
State the question and uncertainty at the precision actually available; do not require the intended breakthrough as an admission condition.

Use both discovery and consolidation work: formulate and test bold candidates, and develop evidence-based small steps in parallel.
Assess an attempt by what it teaches about structures, examples, hypotheses, useful partial results or connections, as well as by improvements in coverage and quantitative bounds.
A round need not produce a theorem or immediate improvement of the final gap estimate.
Exchange useful observations across routes without forcing every direction through S_G0008's current income criterion.
Conjectures, conditional arguments, computations and checked proofs retain their distinct evidential status.

Known negative results constrain their exact assertions and recipes.
Preserve them and use them to design changed experiments, rather than extending them to an entire direction.
Avoid literal repetition of an already settled calculation; a changed hypothesis, object, scale, aggregation or question may justify another test, with the change explained.
Reassess individual experiments and rotate effort when useful; lack of a currently visible end-to-end mechanism alone does not impose an indefinite hold on a candidate direction.

With the natural-log finite-subset entropy in the [Task](../tasks/Yin_T_G0001_v1.md#algebraic-entropy-contract), prove a universal \(\eta>0\) separating every positive entropy from zero, or construct exact positive finite entropies tending to zero.
The group and its torsion-free rank are unrestricted.

The final structural closure criterion is whether finite entropy forces **finite capture**: must there be a subgroup \(V=V(\phi,F)\), generated as a \(\mathbb Z[t]\)-module by one finite set \(F\), such that
\[
h_{\rm alg}(\phi|_V)=h_{\rm alg}(\phi)<\infty?
\]
DGB16's attainment proof isolates this mechanism.
Once capture is obtained, its finite-rank/torsion decomposition and corrected skew-product realization supply a finite realizing set at the audited input scope.
This conditional mechanism does not itself prove capture for all flows.

For refutation, seek a flow with \(0<h_{\rm alg}(\phi)<\infty\) for which **every** finite subset has strictly smaller local entropy.
Such an example refutes the positive gap by contraposition of the valid gap-to-attainment implication.
Finite-entropy torsion flows and torsion-free finite-rank flows already attain, so they are controls, not candidates for this mechanism.

A complementary concrete interface is the entire family \(A\in GL_n(\mathbb Q)\), with \(n\) unbounded, via \(h_{\rm alg}(A)=m(s\chi_A)\).
Preserve denominator content and the leading coefficient; no division by dimension is allowed.
A rank-uniform argument or a certified asymptotic family here could settle the same Task, even if the exploratory language is module-theoretic.

## Inputs and obstruction

- [DGB16-U001](../audits/literature/DGB16/Yin_U001.md#dgb16-u001) supplies finite trajectories, continuity and orbit-generated subgroups; [U002](../audits/literature/DGB16/Yin_U002.md#dgb16-u002) supplies the Addition interface and its retained external boundaries.
- [DGB16-U004](../audits/literature/DGB16/Yin_U004.md#dgb16-u004) checks the infimum equality, finite-rank realization and the repaired attainment argument.
  [GBV15-U001](../audits/literature/GBV15/Yin_U001.md#gbv15-u001) supplies the precise rational formula and realizing sets.
- [DGB16-U003](../audits/literature/DGB16/Yin_U003.md#dgb16-length-function) offers a possible length-function and prime-filtration viewpoint.
  Its original external classification interfaces remain unverified at primary-source level; none is promoted to an accepted new proof input by listing it here.

The main obstruction is uniformity across unbounded rank and algebraic degree.
DGB16 Lemma 7.9 forces stabilization **assuming** a uniform positive gap on successive quotient entropies.
Reusing that assumption as the proof of the desired gap is circular.
Noetherianity of \(\mathbb Z[t]\) controls finitely generated modules; it does not make an arbitrary flow module Noetherian or bound the Mahler weights of all prime quotients.
Uniqueness axioms that already use Mahler normalization do not supply that bound for free.

A direct sum of summands with rigorously summable positive entropies yields nonattainment through finite support, but obtaining arbitrarily small positive summands already contains a gap refutation.
Assuming their existence is a conditional model, not new unconditional evidence.

<a id="method-assessment-2026-10-06"></a>
## Method assessment, 2026-10-06

The [fixed evaluation](../ledger/Yin_L_G0001_v1.md#lehmer-route-assessment) shows why finite capture is a useful final criterion but supplies no reduction of difficulty by itself.
A split semisimple weighted-length model is additive and upper continuous over a Noetherian ring yet fails finite capture.
It is not genuine algebraic entropy; it isolates the need for arithmetic information about entropy weights.
Infinite-rank zero-entropy flows also show why capture must not be confused with finite rank of the entire group.

The concrete hard core can be taken to be irreducible reciprocal noncyclotomic monic units, in unbounded degree: leading coefficients and nonunit norms already give a log(2) bound, while [Smy08-U002, Section 5.1 interface](../audits/literature/Smy08/Yin_U002.md#smy08-reciprocal-number) supplies the nonreciprocal bound at checked statement scope.
Its analytic sketch requires the unit reduction; the original sharp proof remains external.
This does not reduce the target to Salem numbers.
Arithmetic reduction supports the entropy route; it does not change the Task's original full domain.

The uniform-closure method (now organized in uniform-descent criterion), dated 2026-10-02, is adopted as a test of degree-uniformity, not as an available descent theorem.
The shared-structure heuristic (now organized in shared-structure lesson) supports using common orbit relations with G0002; the exact new inequality remains to be found.

<a id="dual-topological-tools"></a>
## Compact dual and topological tools

Pontryagin duality is a tool branch of this same strategy.
For every discrete abelian flow, h_alg(phi)=h_top(phi-hat); retained GBV15 v3 p.30, Theorem 6.12, was checked at statement and proof-outline scope in the additional source note.
The recorded 2026-10-06 source inspection concerned [GBV15 v3](../literature/pdf/GBV15.pdf), p.30, Theorem 6.12.
Root and the assigned entropy agent checked the statement and its proof outline; root inspected the complete rendered page.
The outline invokes torsion duality, addition, direct/inverse-limit continuity and the two Yuzvinski formulas; those external proofs were not newly audited.
No independent full proof of its external inputs is claimed.
A phi-invariant subgroup corresponds to a compact dual factor, so finite capture asks for such a factor carrying the full entropy.
Inverse-limit continuity gives a supremum and does not make that supremum a maximum.

For A in GL_d(Z), the induced toral automorphism has entropy m(chi_A), without an expansiveness assumption ([Smy08-U004](../audits/literature/Smy08/Yin_U004.md#smy08-toral-entropy), Section 14.2, printed p.340 / PDF p.19, external theorem at checked statement scope).
Thus unbounded-dimensional toral automorphisms already carry the hard monic-unit family.
Expansive symbolic coding or homoclinic tools must be checked separately on the nonexpansive unit-circle cases; restricting to expansive systems loses part of the target.
General rational duals Q^d-hat must not be replaced by tori.

The [arithmetic route](Yin_S_G0004.md#periodic-resultant-interface) supplies the precise toral periodic-point/resultant interface and a counterexample to its extension to all compact abelian groups.
Use periodic counts to seek new arithmetic restrictions on entropy, not to replace entropy outside that checked scope.

For each fixed q>=2, the computable positive total-Mahler gap is uniform over unbounded periods p, original degrees D and coefficients, provided the exact reciprocal lift has energy at most q^2 and D>J(q)p.
The [orbitwise minimum and binary criterion](../claims/Yin_C_G0847_v1.md#reciprocal-orbit-minimum-energy) make the energy condition decidable, and C842 NG supplies infinitely many actual inputs in every admissible class, including classes with genuinely unbounded least periods.
The [coverage boundary](Yin_S_G0004.md#unbounded-period-energy-frontier) retains fixed q and both paid hypotheses; no numerical execution, arbitrary-pattern coverage or unrestricted Lehmer conclusion follows.

<a id="finite-support-phase-frontier"></a>
## Finite-support phase separation and its coverage boundary

[Adopted C848](../claims/Yin_C_G0848_v1.md#decision-whole-reviewed-2026-10-28), through [whole independent R1068](../audits/reviews/Yin_R_G1068_v1.md#whole-finite-support-phase-finding), replaces C845's sufficient n>kJ test by an exact finite support noncollision condition for the same allowed fixed-q low-energy seeds.
Its [support-defined set Bad(H)](../claims/Yin_C_G0848_v1.md#phase-support-and-exceptional-shifts) has at most N_q=(4q^4)^J positive shifts, independently of the seed degree, and is common to both signs; exclusion from this set supplies exact constant-term preservation.
For a supplied good shift and full integral value, including an initially possible zero value, the original window n+k<=C*d gives m(P)>u_q/C, with every original norm and finite Fourier cost retained.
The [arithmetic frontier](Yin_S_G0004.md#finite-support-phase-frontier) retains the many-distinct-shift and support-lattice sufficient conditions without assuming the required integral shifts exist.
Both concrete nonempty comparison classes already have C93/C95 coverage, so they illustrate the broader support test rather than new arithmetic coverage of those classes or an unrestricted Mahler conclusion.

<a id="fixed-bivariate-template-frontier"></a>
## Fixed finite templates at every positive exponent ratio

[Adopted C849](../claims/Yin_C_G0849_v1.md#decision-whole-reviewed-2026-10-28), through [whole independent R1069](../audits/reviews/Yin_R_G1069_v1.md#whole-all-ratio-template-finding), covers every positive integer pair n,g for a fixed finite integer Laurent-template family with m_2(F)<log q.
The actual [specialization class](../claims/Yin_C_G0849_v1.md#fixed-bivariate-template-specialization-class) requires V=F(x^g,x^n) nonzero, V/q coefficientwise nonintegral, full algebraic integrality of V(alpha)/q in the original unit field and the true span window T<=C*d.
It gives T*m(P)/d>a(q,Fset)>0 and hence m(P)>a/C, with an effective numerical gap when C is computable or a computable upper bound is supplied.
The [fixed monic reversal corollary](../claims/Yin_C_G0849_v1.md#fixed-reversal-all-ratio-corollary) covers both signs with a single-prime constant option; it does not supply arbitrary-input certificates or remove C847's repetition condition for unbounded seed families.
The [arithmetic scope](Yin_S_G0004.md#fixed-bivariate-template-frontier) and [complete costs](Yin_S_G0005.md#fixed-bivariate-template-frontier) remain fixed-family conditional results, not an unrestricted Mahler conclusion.

<a id="finite-fourier-budget-frontier"></a>
## Explicit scalar and phase budgets with the actual mean

[Adopted C851](../claims/Yin_C_G0851_v1.md#decision-whole-reviewed-2026-10-28), through [whole independent R1071](../audits/reviews/Yin_R_G1071_v1.md#whole-finite-fourier-budget-finding), replaces the available angular coefficient expense by its finite true-term bound Lambda_(N,J), while keeping the actual circle constant and every original norm, radial, frequency and matrix cost.
It proves the explicit positive rational cutoffs u_s(q)=q^(-256q^2) and u_p(q)=q^(-512q^2) for the exact scalar and phase hypotheses, without asserting that they pass the older2^J tests.
The [scalar consequences](../claims/Yin_C_G0851_v1.md#explicit-scalar-cutoff-and-rigidity) retain arbitrary final support, exact signed-monomial equality and own-span positive real-weight families; the [phase consequences](../claims/Yin_C_G0851_v1.md#explicit-phase-cutoff-and-noncollision) retain the allowed seeds and supplied good shifts or large separation.
The [sharper support-exception count](../claims/Yin_C_G0851_v1.md#sharper-support-exception-count) improves the sufficient number of supplied distinct integral shifts for the same seed without producing those shifts.
The [arithmetic frontier](Yin_S_G0004.md#finite-fourier-budget-frontier) retains actual integrality and the original window, and the growing-denominator conclusion is conditional on supplied certificates rather than a gap uniform over arbitrary q.
The C168/C145 mechanisms are credited with their historical unknown review relationships unchanged; no source-proof expansion, numerical execution, automatic certificate coverage or unrestricted G0001 conclusion is selected.
